Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种风险受控的 LLM 裁判框架：先用参数知识评估，高置信才接受；低置信则转向网页检索增强评估，并通过 Clopper--Pearson 校准双阈值，保证已接受判决的错误率不超过给定水平，同时提高可判定覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为裁判已广泛用于大规模评估开放式输出，但在无参考答案的客观任务中，单靠参数知识可能幻觉或证据不足，单靠工具增强又带来成本与调用策略问题；更关键的是，两者都缺乏对已接受判决错误风险的形式化控制。

Method: 提出风险受控的双阈值框架：在留出校准集上用有限样本 Clopper--Pearson 区间校准不确定性阈值，使已接受判决中的假发现率以高概率低于用户指定水平 α。若参数模式置信度不足，则路由到检索增强模式，由裁判收集网页证据并在第二个校准阈值下重新评估。

Result: 在开放域问答基准和不同规模裁判模型上，该框架能维持目标错误率，同时相比单一模式基线获得显著更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 通过校准参数模式与检索增强模式的两个接受阈值，论文证明可在无需额外假设的情况下将有限样本风险控制保证扩展到路由式评估流程，从而兼顾可靠性与覆盖率。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IOL-AI Challenge，用国际语言学奥赛未公开题评测大模型。结果显示前沿模型可达金牌水平，但受限系统表现很弱；模型规模不是关键，解码与输出处理更重要。自动评分能正确排序但分数尺度失真，语言学谜题可作为通用推理能力的重要基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型推理研究主要集中在数学和代码等规则已给定的领域；语言学谜题要求模型先发现隐藏规则再进行推理，因此更能检验可泛化推理能力。

Method: 提出 IOL-AI Challenge：基于 2026 年国际语言学奥林匹克个人赛未公开题目开展开放科学竞赛，在严格算力预算下收集系统提交；同时由官方 IOL 评委按人类选手标准评分，并对 15 个不受限前沿与开放模型进行基准测试，比较自动指标与人工评分。

Result: 竞赛收到 46 支队伍 731 次提交。Claude Opus 4.8 的评委得分达到相当于金牌水平；作者提交的两个受限系统仅处于人类参赛者后 5% 区间。14B 模型可超过两倍规模模型，提升主要来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量。自动指标与评委排序完全一致，但压缩分数尺度：弱系统约高估 13 分，强系统被低估。前沿模型即使可能具备部分语言背景知识，也未显著帮助解题。

Conclusion: 语言学推理任务由于要求发现规则并应用规则，是评估大语言模型通用、可迁移推理能力的强基准；模型规模并非决定性因素，系统设计中的解码和输出处理同样关键。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chain-of-Experience，让 LLM 在测试时通过自反馈和环境反馈进行多轮经验积累，从而提升数学、代码和知识任务表现；实验显示该方法普遍优于无反馈推理，提升准确率、降低 API 成本，并且早期迭代即可获得主要收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评测通常关注零样本或单次推理表现，忽视了模型在测试时通过交互反馈从经验中持续改进的能力；论文希望刻画并评估这种类人式的迭代学习机制。

Method: 提出 Chain-of-Experience（CoE）测试时学习设置：模型在多轮交互中积累经验轨迹，并利用自反馈或环境反馈（如答案正确性、公开代码测试通过率）形成持续改进循环。作者在数学、编程和知识任务上，使用包括 GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet 在内的 8 个 LLM 进行评估，并比较无反馈基线及其他测试时策略。

Result: CoE 在各任务和模型上稳定优于无反馈基线；仅使用自反馈也能带来显著提升。总体上实现 5.6% 的性能提升，同时 API 成本降低 19%。组合互补反馈通道可进一步提升效果；相比已有测试时策略，CoE 具有更高的 token 效率。模型基础能力与改进能力呈正相关；在弱反馈或伪反馈下仍具鲁棒性，且大部分收益出现在早期迭代。

Conclusion: 论文表明，LLM 不应只按静态零样本能力评估；通过测试时的迭代经验积累，模型可以在不更新参数的情况下持续改进。CoE 是一种有效、成本更低且 token 效率更高的推理时增强范式，尤其适合具有可反馈信号的复杂任务。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张语言意义不仅有“关联强度”（振幅），还需要“相位”这一第二参数，用来解释同一意义在不同历史、个体和关系中如何改变甚至反转其贡献；现有语料平均和标准 Transformer 会抹去这种信息，因此未来模型应具备主体索引、历史敏感、相位承载的语义状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建模主要依赖词共现、词嵌入和注意力权重来估计意义之间的关联强度，作者认为这只能描述“振幅”，无法解释同一意义在不同个体、关系和历史语境中如何以相反方式参与解释。论文试图补充一个被群体语料平均化抹去的关系性参数。

Method: 论文提出概念性理论框架，将语言意义关系分解为振幅与相位两个参数；论证标准 Transformer、静态语料统计和以单义性为目标的可解释性方法无法显式表示相位；并提出六类可检验预测，用于区分被抑制意义是否仍保持激活、相遇顺序是否改变短语作用、语言标记是否改变共享短语理解，以及带历史信息的模型是被历史改变还是仅获得历史描述。

Result: 论文得出弱主张：解释需要第二个关系参数，即相位；它是有符号、持久且索引到个体与二元关系的语义状态。量子概率可作为该参数的一种记号系统，但论文不声称大脑中存在量子过程。强主张，即量子演算比有符号经典模型更能约束这些现象，目前仍依赖尚未推导完成的相遇顺序约束。

Conclusion: 作者认为语言模型若要捕捉讽刺、典故、引用等依赖意义共存与反转的现象，需要从群体平均语料转向带有主体索引、关系历史和相位承载语义状态的架构。当前标准 Transformer 在冻结推理阶段缺乏这种显式机制。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TokEval，用多种语言学和结构化内在指标评估分词器，并通过受控预训练验证这些指标与下游能力的关系；结果表明信息论指标更预测语言建模，结构指标更关联数学和代码等任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型分词器通常缺乏系统评估，但其设计会直接影响模型能力；现有研究对哪些分词器属性会影响哪些下游表现理解不足。

Method: 提出 TokEval 分词器评估框架，设计超越 fertility 和压缩率的内在指标，覆盖语言学与结构属性，如 UTF-8 字符边界完整性、数学中的数字位值边界对齐等；通过受控预训练实验，仅改变分词器训练数据混合、预分词策略和训练算法，并在 bits-per-byte、语言理解、数学推理和代码生成基准上评估。

Result: 实验发现不同分词器内在属性对应不同模型能力：信息论指标能较好预测语言建模能力，Spearman 相关系数最高达 0.80；结构敏感指标如数字和换行处理与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval 可用于更有原则地评估分词器，并在内在指标与下游表现一致时，部分替代昂贵的预训练 sweep。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体 AI 系统，用于 CT 放射科报告的结构化和质量检查。系统可保留原文内容并按解剖部位重组 Findings，同时识别报告不一致和潜在质量问题；医师评估显示其结构化和 QA 表现总体良好，未发现重要信息遗漏或虚构内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科报告通常包含非结构化叙述，可能存在解剖分区不统一、Findings 与 Impression 不一致、性别-解剖冲突及危急值沟通记录缺失等质量问题。研究旨在开发一种本地部署的多智能体 AI 系统，在保护本地数据环境的同时实现报告结构化与质量保证。

Method: 研究回顾性纳入2023—2024年15名认证放射科医师出具的638份胸部、腹部和盆腔 CT 报告。研究者构建多智能体 AI 流水线，使用正则规则和本地大语言模型在句子层面将 Findings 部分整理为标准化解剖章节，并检测 Findings 与 Impression 或同一章节内部的不匹配、性别-解剖冲突、危急发现沟通未记录等问题。两名认证放射科医师独立评估45份报告子集。

Result: 系统成功将全部报告 Findings 部分的22,270个句子结构化为预定义解剖格式，同时保留原始报告内容。系统共标记90份报告（14.1%），最常见问题为章节不匹配（80份，12.5%）。在45份报告评估中，两名医师一致认为31份（69%）重构正确、2份（4%）重构错误，对12份（27%）存在分歧；两名评审均认为没有遗漏临床重要信息，也没有生成虚假内容。整体 QA 表现在84%的报告中被评为“优秀”或“良好”，其余为“一般”。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体 AI 系统能够在同一工作流中结合放射科报告结构化与质量保证，并在放射科医师评估中表现良好。该类系统有望促进放射科报告标准化和临床质量控制。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Expected free energy as an information constraint on the Bethe Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17167)
*Wouter M. Kouw*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出约束 Bethe 自由能主动推断框架，用互信息约束维持探索动机，使主动推断更适合消息传递；在特定乘子下可恢复 EFE，并能通过信息需求调节认知驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推断通过最小化未来预测上的期望自由能选择行动，但对未观测结果取期望会破坏自由能的 KL 结构，使基于消息传递的推断处理变得困难。

Method: 提出一种基于 Bethe 自由能的替代表述，并在归一化、边缘化和形式约束之外加入信息约束：给定行动时，未来观测、状态与参数之间的互信息至少应达到目标先验的熵。通过分析带 Karush-Kuhn-Tucker 乘子的约束 Bethe 拉格朗日量的驻点，将其与期望自由能解联系起来，并在三个任务上与 EFE 和 Q-MDP 比较。

Result: 当 KKT 乘子取特定值时，约束 Bethe 拉格朗日量的驻点可恢复期望自由能解。随着信息需求变化，求得的乘子会经历非活跃、内部和饱和三种状态；非活跃状态下认知驱动力完全关闭，饱和状态下认知驱动力达到最大。实验在三个任务中评估了该约束 Bethe agent 相对 EFE 与 Q-MDP 的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，可用带信息约束的 Bethe 自由能框架替代传统期望自由能表述，从而保留主动推断中的认知探索动机，同时支持消息传递式推断，并提供对认知驱动力强弱的可控调节机制。

Abstract: Active inference selects actions by minimising an expected free energy functional over predicted futures. However, adding an expectation over yet-unobserved outcomes means the free energy functional no longer has a Kullback-Leibler structure, which hinders message passing treatments of inference procedures. We propose an alternative formulation based on a Bethe free energy functional, fully supporting inference by message passing. The epistemic drive is maintained by imposing an information constraint, next to normalisation, marginalisation and form constraints, insisting that the mutual information between future observations, states and parameters given actions must be at least as large as the entropy of the goal prior. For a specific value of the corresponding Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multiplier, the stationary point of this constrained Bethe Lagrangian recovers the expected free energy solution. We show that, as the information demand is varied, the solved multiplier moves through its inactive, interior, and saturated regimes. In the inactive regime the agent's epistemic drive switches off entirely, while in the saturated regime it is maximal. We compare the performance of the constrained Bethe agent on three tasks against EFE and Q-MDP.

</details>


### [140] [Chi-Squared Geometry for Robust Finite-Blocklength Information and Dispersion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17305)
*Hassan Tavakoli,Thinh Nguyen,Bella Bose*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为接近完全噪声的离散无记忆信道建立了一套按列卡方几何，用一个偏差参数 η 在不计算信道矩阵对数的情况下，紧致估计互信息、信道离散度和有限码长设计速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散无记忆信道的互信息、信道离散度和有限码长速率通常涉及对信道矩阵取对数，计算与认证较复杂。本文关注接近完全噪声信道的情形，希望构造一种无需逐项求对数、但仍能给出紧致界的几何分析框架。

Method: 提出按列定义的卡方几何，用参数 η 表征转移概率相对输出边缘分布的最坏偏差；在 η 较小时建立互信息与 χ² 量之间的三阶比值展开，推导信道离散度与 χ² 量的双边等价界，并据此构造带显式认证误差的有限码长鲁棒设计速率。

Result: 得到三项主要结果：I(X;Y)/χ²(X;Y) 在 η→0 时趋于 1/2，并具有 O(η) 的偏度修正；V(X;Y) 可由 χ²(X;Y) 上下夹逼，常数 c±(η) 随 η→0 收敛到 1；认证速率 R_cert(n,ε) 的总认证间隙为 O(η)+O(η/√n)+O(log n/n)。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在信道接近完全噪声时，卡方几何可以有效替代对数型信息量分析，为互信息、离散度和有限码长编码速率提供紧致、显式且计算友好的认证界。

Abstract: We develop a column-wise chi-squared geometry for discrete memoryless channels (DMCs) yielding tight, logarithm-free bounds on mutual information, channel dispersion, and finite-blocklength coding rates without evaluating logarithms of the channel matrix. The key parameter is~\(η\)---the worst-case relative deviation of a transition probability from its output marginal, which is small precisely when the channel is close to the fully noisy channel $t_{ij}=s_j$. We prove three main results: (1) a third-order ratio expansion showing \(I(X;Y)/χ^2(X;Y)\to 1/2\) as \(η\to 0\) with an \(O(η)\) skewness correction; (2) a two-sided dispersion equivalence bounding \(V(X;Y)\) above and below by \(χ^2(X;Y)\) with explicit constants \(c_{\pm}(η)\to 1\); and (3) a certified robust design rate \(R_{\mathrm{cert}}(n,\varepsilon)\) with total certification gap \(O(η)+O(η/\sqrt{n})+O(\log n/n)\). The certified bounds on \(I\) and \(V\) require only addition, multiplication, division, and square roots; the final rate also uses \(Q^{-1}(\varepsilon)\).

</details>


### [141] [Universal CKM for Environment-Aware Wireless Networks: Enabling Cross-Device and Cross-Task Channel Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17382)
*Haiquan Lu,Yong Zeng,Cheng-Xiang Wang,Xiqi Gao,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出通用信道知识地图uCKM，将其作为6G环境感知网络的通用无线环境先验，用于跨设备、跨任务复用信道知识；文章分析现有CKM局限，提出“All for uCKM / uCKM for All”范式，并用仿真展示其可行性和性能收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道知识地图（CKM）通常与特定无线设备和下游任务强绑定，导致其在6G环境感知无线网络中的可扩展性、复用性和跨场景迁移能力受限。论文希望构建一种可作为无线环境先验的通用CKM，以支持跨设备、跨任务的信道知识迁移。

Method: 文章提出通用信道知识地图（uCKM）概念，首先回顾代表性CKM并分析局限；随后提出uCKM驱动的环境感知无线网络新范式，并从构建阶段和利用阶段提出“All for uCKM”和“uCKM for All”两种愿景；进一步讨论uCKM面临的主要挑战及潜在解决方案，并通过仿真验证其可行性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，uCKM能够作为共享的无线环境先验，在不同设备和任务之间实现信道知识迁移，并带来性能增益，说明其在环境感知6G网络中的应用具有可行性。

Conclusion: uCKM有望突破传统CKM对设备和任务的耦合限制，成为6G环境感知无线网络中的基础性知识表示框架。通过汇聚多源数据并服务多类任务，uCKM可提升网络可扩展性、可复用性和智能化水平，但仍需解决构建、泛化、标准化和隐私等挑战。

Abstract: Channel knowledge map (CKM) is a promising technology for environment-aware sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks. However, most existing CKMs are tightly coupled with wireless devices and downstream tasks, which limit their scalability and reusability in wireless networks. To address these limitations, this article proposes the concept of universal CKM (uCKM) as a foundational wireless environment prior, which aims to enable cross-device and cross-task channel knowledge transfer for environment-aware wireless networks. We first revisit the representative CKMs and discuss their limitations. Then, the uCKM-enabled new paradigm for environment-aware wireless networks is introduced, and its benefits are highlighted from the perspectives of uCKM construction and utilization phases, for which we propose the visions of ``All for uCKM'' and ``uCKM for All'', i.e., the data acquired by all devices and tasks should contribute to the construction of uCKM, and vice versa. Subsequently, we discuss the main challenges of uCKM and propose potential solutions. Last, we provide simulation results to demonstrate the feasibility and performance gains brought by uCKM and outline future research directions.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic Question Design for Efficient Estimation of Aggregate Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17459)
*Kazuyoshi Fukuda,Masaki Inoue*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应问卷方法：用贝叶斯/粒子滤波估计人类偏好，用期望信息增益和 epsilon-greedy 选择问题；理论证明其误差与搜索效率优势，并通过仿真验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效估计群体层面的人类偏好，并根据受访者已有回答动态调整后续问题，以减少无效提问、提升偏好估计效率。

Method: 论文将问题分解为偏好估计与问题设计两个子问题。偏好估计方面，建立受访者偏好模型，采用贝叶斯估计，并用粒子滤波进行计算上高效的近似；问题设计方面，将问题选择建模为期望信息增益最大化问题，并使用 epsilon-greedy 策略进行高效求解。

Result: 理论上，论文基于信息论方法分析偏好估计误差，并推导误差下界；同时分析问题搜索效率，证明所提 epsilon-greedy 方法比随机搜索更高效。数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合贝叶斯偏好估计、粒子滤波近似以及基于期望信息增益的自适应问题设计，可以更高效地估计聚合人类偏好，并优于随机提问策略。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of efficiently estimating aggregate human preferences by dynamically adapting questions based on respondents'answers. To this end, we formulate and address two sub-problems: preference estimation and question design. First, regarding preference estimation, we model respondents' preferences and estimate them using Bayesian estimation, employing a particle filter as a computationally efficient approximation. The main theoretical contribution to this sub-problem is to analyze the preference estimation error using an information-theoretic approach, deriving a theoretical lower bound for the error. Second, regarding question design, we formulate the design problem as an Expected Information Gain maximization problem and employ an epsilon-greedy strategy to solve the problem in a computationally efficient way. We theoretically analyze the search efficiency of the approach, demonstrating that it achieves higher efficiency than a random search. Finally, we verify the effectiveness of the proposed method through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [143] [Learnware for CSI Feedback: Scene-specific Small Models Can Do Big](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17760)
*Xiangyi Li,Jiajia Guo,Chao-Kai Wen,Xin Geng,Shi Jin,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一个 Learnware 增强的 CSI 模型仓库框架：基站用本地数据分布指纹检索最合适的预训练模型，而无需上传原始 CSI。该方法在保持隐私和降低训练成本的同时，提高了 LOS/NLOS 场景下的反馈性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来 6G 系统需要高容量与高频谱效率，智能 CSI 反馈是关键；但现有深度学习方法在泛化能力与场景专用性能之间存在矛盾：大模型泛化较好但计算和调参成本高，小模型适配特定环境但每个基站都需重复进行昂贵的端到端训练。

Method: 提出基于模型仓库的部署框架，由集中式 AI 数据中心维护场景特定 CSI 模型目录；结合 Learnware 框架，为每个模型配置语义规格（网络结构参数）和统计规格（训练数据分布的 codebook-fingerprint 嵌入）。基站仅上传本地统计规格，用于检索最相关的预训练模型；同时设计数据驱动搜索策略，将 codebook fingerprints 与模型性能进行匹配。

Result: 所提搜索策略实现超过 90% 的模型选择准确率。仿真结果显示，相比 General Model，该方案在 LOS 和 NLOS 场景下分别带来 18.8% 和 57.7% 的性能提升，并最多减少 1000 个本地微调样本和 100 个训练 epoch。

Conclusion: 该 Learnware-based CSI 模型复用方案能够减少冗余训练、提升场景适配性能、降低通信与检索开销，并通过避免上传原始 CSI 数据增强隐私保护，适合 6G CSI 反馈模型的快速部署。

Abstract: Intelligent channel state information (CSI) feedback is essential for realizing the high capacity and spectral efficiency goals of future 6G systems, yet existing deep learning solutions face a trade-off between model generalization and scenario-specific performance. Large neural networks generalize well but incur high computational and tuning costs, while small models excel in particular environments but require repetitive costly end-to-end training for each base station (BS). To address these challenges, we introduce a model repository-based deployment framework in which a centralized AI data center maintains a catalog of scene-specific CSI models. The repository is enhanced with a Learnware-based framework, where each model is associated with a specification including semantic part (network architecture parameters) and statistical part (codeboo-fingerprint embeddings of training-data distributions). A BS submits only its local statistical specifications to retrieve the most relevant pre-trained model, enhancing data privacy by avoiding raw CSI transmission and drastically reducing retrieval latency and communication overhead. We further develop a data-driven search strategy that matches codebook fingerprints to model performance, achieving over 90% selection accuracy. In simulations, our scheme yields 18.8% and 57.7% performance improvements over the General Model in LOS and NLOS scenarios, respectively while reducing local fine-tuning by up to 1000 samples and 100 epochs. This Learnware-based approach minimizes redundant training, maximizes model reuse, and supports rapid,privacy-enhancing deployment of CSI feedback models.

</details>


### [144] [On the Estimation of Chernoff Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17916)
*Kadircan Aksoy,Peter Jung*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出用 kNN 直接估计 Chernoff information 优化条件中的导数，再用二分法寻找最优参数，并证明导数估计器的 L2 一致性，从而构造具有理论保证的非参数 Chernoff information 估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chernoff information 在贝叶斯二元假设检验中刻画最优错误指数，但其非参数估计困难，因为它需要在阶数参数上优化未归一化 Rényi 散度，且闭式解只存在于少数简单参数族。

Method: 论文将原始优化问题改写为一阶导数为零的条件，通过直接估计该导数来定位最优混合参数；具体使用 k 近邻方法构造导数估计器，并结合二分搜索将最优参数逼近到任意精度。

Result: 作者证明了在密度及其定义域满足温和正则条件下，所提出的导数估计器具有 L2 一致性；由此得到一个基于导数估计和二分法的 Chernoff information 非参数估计器。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Chernoff information 提供了一种理论上有一致性保证的非参数估计途径，避免了直接估计并优化未归一化 Rényi 散度的困难。

Abstract: Chernoff information is a fundamental divergence measure characterizing the optimal error exponent in Bayesian binary hypothesis testing, with applications in information fusion, time-series analysis, and statistical learning theory. However, closed-form expressions exist only for simple parametric families, and nonparametric estimation remains difficult because the quantity is defined as an optimization of the unnormalized Rényi divergence over its order. We reformulate this optimization via a derivative condition, whose zero locates the optimal mixture parameter, and estimate the derivative directly using a $k$-nearest-neighbor method. We prove the $L_2$-consistency of the derivative estimator under mild regularity conditions on the densities and their domain. Coupled with a bisection procedure that locates the optimal parameter up to arbitrary precision, this yields an estimator for Chernoff information.

</details>


### [145] [Tail exponents of conditional guesswork via the method of types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17949)
*Adway Girish,Andreina Patrizia Motter,Emre Telatar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文用类型计数分析随机序列猜测问题，扩展到含相关边信息的条件情形，推导出基于条件倾斜分布散度的显式猜测指数，并应用于带边信息的密码暴力破解分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在存在逐元素相关边信息时，对独立同分布随机序列实现值进行猜测的理论问题，并刻画猜测次数尾概率与指数行为。

Method: 采用类型计数方法，先重新推导无边信息情形下猜测次数尾概率估计，再将该计数论证扩展到条件设置，分析条件倾斜分布相关的散度表达式。

Result: 得到无边信息情形下与大偏差方法一致的尾概率估计，并在有边信息情形下给出新的显式猜测工作量指数表达式。

Conclusion: 类型计数为猜测问题提供了简洁且可扩展的分析框架，能够统一处理无边信息与有边信息场景，并可用于密码暴力猜测等实际问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of guessing a realization of an i.i.d. random sequence given element-wise correlated side-information. We use type-counting to provide estimates of the tail probabilities of the number of guesses for the case without side-information, which was shown earlier through large-deviation techniques. We then extend the same counting argument to the conditional setting, obtaining new explicit expressions for the corresponding guesswork exponents as divergences involving conditional tilted distributions. Finally, we provide an application of these exponents to brute-force password guessing with side-information.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GxP-Agent，用 DAG 组织多智能体临床试验编程流程，在 CDISC 数据集生成任务上显著优于单智能体、平铺多智能体和检索增强基线，并在 ADSL 与 ADAE 上实现 100% 结构匹配，表明流程知识的图结构编码能显著提升 LLM 在监管级数据编程中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验编程需要将研究方案转换为符合 CDISC 标准的分析就绪数据集，是监管申报中的关键瓶颈；现有 LLM 单次代码生成在该任务上可靠性极差，无法生成有效的受试者级分析数据集。

Method: 提出 GxP-Agent 多智能体系统，将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），把单体数据集生成任务分解为 15 个领域特定节点，由具备 pharmaverse 技能上下文的 worker agents 执行，并结合验证门控与条件重试。在新构建的执行式基准 CDISC-Bench 上评估，该基准基于 FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01。

Result: 在 ADSL 任务上，GxP-Agent + Claude Sonnet 4.6 三次独立运行均达到 100% 结构匹配（49/49 变量、254 条正确记录），显著优于最佳检索增强基线的 59.2%，而单智能体和平铺式多智能体方法均为 0%。DAG 架构也提升较弱模型表现：GPT-4.1 在同一 DAG 下达到 59.2% 平均结构匹配，而在其他架构下均为 0%。该方法还泛化到 ADAE 任务，首次尝试即达到 100% 结构匹配（55 变量、1,191 条记录）。

Conclusion: 将领域流程知识显式编码为图拓扑结构，而不是仅依赖 LLM 自身推理，是实现可靠、符合 GxP 要求的临床试验编程的关键。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [147] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aegis 将 AI 工具调用从“模型直接执行”改为“模型提案、可信运行时裁决”，在沙箱实验中成功阻止风险动作产生受治理副作用，但结论仅限于该实验范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI 系统会请求可产生真实操作副作用的工具动作，仅靠提示词治理无法形成可靠执行边界，因此需要一种在工具执行前拦截并裁决动作的运行时安全机制。

Method: 论文提出 Aegis 运行时治理系统，将模型输出视为“动作提案”，由可信决策层在执行前依据活动策略状态进行评估；系统在服务端解析来源信息，在不确定时默认拒绝，并对特定情形采用 Senate-style quorum 授权机制。实验在重复沙箱语料上进行，覆盖 5 个运行族、42 个任务、3 种条件，每族重复 10 次，共分析 6,300 行数据。

Result: 提示词策略条件下出现 79 行风险比较路径泄漏；而在 2,100 行 Aegis 治理数据中，记录到 0 次受治理 mock-tool 应用和 0 次受治理风险副作用完成。1,832 行 Aegis 尝试治理记录均保留可信来源解析，1,019 行 Senate 裁决记录均具备 quorum 与最终签名计票证据。

Conclusion: 实验结果不证明通用自主智能体安全，但支持一个更窄的系统性结论：在该沙箱语料与评估设置中，运行时动作边界治理能阻止已观察到的风险提案转化为受治理的实际副作用。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [148] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较 Sonnet 5 在 AIME 2026 上显式 high effort 与省略 effort 的 API 合约效果：high effort 成本更高，但准确率未显示显著提升；省略 effort 的每正确答案成本更低。研究还指出 effort 省略语义依赖具体模型和提供商，结论仅适用于实验设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API 购买者实际购买的是带日期的调用合约，而不只是模型名称；其中 reasoning-effort 条款可能影响成本与性能，但其省略时的语义和实际效果并不清晰。论文旨在实证检验显式 high effort 与省略 effort 在同一模型、同一任务上的差异。

Method: 作者对 Sonnet 5 进行预注册配对对比实验：使用 30 道 AIME 2026 题目，每题调用 5 次，比较显式 high effort 合约与省略 effort 合约。每次付费尝试被分配固定终端类别；统计推断以题目为重采样单位并保留重复调用结构。同时进行带日期合约普查、Models-API 元数据检查和预注册原始响应探针，以分析不同模型/提供商下 effort 省略的语义。

Result: 显式 high effort 每次调用的平均交付成本比省略 effort 高 0.01031 美元，区间为 [+0.00204, +0.01974]。准确率差异为 +0.0133，区间为 [-0.0267, +0.0467]，未检测到显著准确率差异，但不能排除最高约 4.67 个百分点的收益。按注册点估计，high-effort 合约每个正确答案成本为 0.08665 美元，省略 effort 合约为 0.07662 美元。

Conclusion: 在该模型、任务和采集日期范围内，显式 high effort 明确增加成本，但没有检测到可靠的准确率提升；省略 effort 的合约在成本效率上更优。论文强调 reasoning-effort 的省略语义具有模型和提供商特异性，因此相关结论应限定在具体合约、模型、任务和日期条件下。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [149] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedPref 用联邦偏好学习训练结构化放射学信息抽取模型，在不共享报告或标注的前提下提升多机构尤其小数据站点性能，但仍落后于集中式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本描述发现及其位置，但下游检索与分析需要固定结构化模式；同时，不同机构标注数据分布不均，小医院本地证据少，而直接汇聚数据又可能不可行。

Method: 提出 FedPref：使用冻结的公共语言模型生成候选 JSON 抽取结果，由本地标注对候选进行排序；各机构仅共享模型更新，协同训练紧凑的 Qwen3-8B 适配器。为避免单模型重复采样导致候选塌缩，引入异构教师模型池提供跨模型对比。

Result: 在六个模拟医院的开发数据上，FedPref 相比各站点独立训练，客户端平均 F1 提升 2.49 点，最差站点 F1 提升 9.10 点，低数据量站点收益最大。集中式 pooled preference-pair 训练的客户端平均 F1 仍高 2.66 点。在 400 份人工验证金标准测试集上，FedPref 达到 68.68 F1，集中训练达到 71.67，排序一致。

Conclusion: FedPref 使数据规模和分布不均、且无法共享原始报告或标注的机构仍能通过联邦协作获益，但其性能仍略低于集中式汇聚训练。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [150] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FAR 人机协作流程，让专家只需提供研究方向，AI 从文献中自动寻找开放问题、尝试解决并推荐给专家审查；组合数学实验显示该方法能从大规模文献中筛出高价值数学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 系统在数学研究中的能力提升，前沿模型推理与专家数学审查都成为稀缺资源。现有 AI 辅助数学流程通常依赖专家预先选择问题并在最后审查结果，这两个环节逐渐成为研究级数学发现的瓶颈。

Method: 论文提出一种新的“人类给出研究方向、AI 自动寻找问题并筛选结果”的协作范式，并构建 Find, Attempt, and Recommend（FAR）级联流程：从文献语料中发现候选猜想或开放问题，过滤出表述良好且仍未解决的问题，再通过模型推理、自动分诊和推荐机制筛选出值得专家审查的潜在成果。

Result: 在组合数学试点中，系统从 5,245 篇论文中恢复 6,453 个候选猜想或开放问题，过滤得到 4,717 个看似表述良好且仍开放的猜想；随后推理与自动分诊产生 598 个潜在解决方案，并选择 77 项供作者团队审查。最终识别出多个有趣发现，包括关于 Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts、Erdős--Straus、Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova 和 Lund--Saraf--Wolf 等人的猜想与问题的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将专家输入从“指定单个问题”转变为“给定研究方向”，并用 AI 在文献中自动发现、尝试和推荐问题，可以更高效地分配模型推理与专家审查资源，推动 AI 辅助数学发现。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [151] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillEffect，在语言模型生成工具调用代码前加入可检查的 lowering 与有界执行机制，防止语义正确但内存失控的程序执行。通过插件化关系定义和共享运行时，它在多类算子中降低内存峰值、提升完成率，并能拒绝恶意配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills 会把工具使用的过程性与资源约束交给语言模型实例化为具体程序；但模型为既有工具接口生成代码时，即使语义正确，也可能一次性加载完整输入而超过单次工具调用的内存上限。因此需要一种在授权执行前可检查、可控内存、可审计的降级执行机制。

Method: 提出 SkillEffect：一种 checked-lowering runtime。其核心是通过独立检查器基于提交程序和不可变输入重建 lowering；每个 relation plugin 提供源识别器、输入事实抽取器、有界 IR 构造器、arena-bound 函数和输出后置条件；共享运行时负责 checked selection、有界 VM 执行、原子容量租赁与 staged publication。

Result: 在六类算子上，有界访问显著降低峰值内存，并在外部固定内存上限下提升完成率。六个插件覆盖五种执行模式，包括流式归约和 bounded-heap Top-k。XLSX onboarding 与 Top-k 扩展表明新关系和新 retained-state 模式可复用同一信任边界；检查器接受所有合法配置并拒绝所有对抗性 proposal。

Conclusion: SkillEffect 证明了一个统一的 checked-lowering 架构可以在 Agent 工具调度阶段，对异构的已注册内存关系实施可审计、可恢复且有界的资源控制。其通用性来自架构复用，而非自动支持任意计算；每种新计算仍需经过审计的 relation plugin。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [152] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“记忆—信号前沿”来描述有界智能体在记忆和通信受限时达到任务表现所需的资源权衡；初步结果表明重复历史可减少消息长度，但隐藏规律未必能被利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界智能体在决策时可依赖自身历史记忆、同伴通信，或二者结合；在记忆与通信资源均受限时，关键问题是如何分配信息预算以达到给定任务表现。

Method: 论文提出在固定任务与决策规则下，用“可达区域”刻画满足性能阈值的记忆率—消息率组合，并将其有效边界定义为 remembering--signaling frontier。作者还通过初步指称游戏观察目标重复与隐藏周期规则对消息长度的影响，并建议通过系统操控记忆率与消息率来估计该前沿。

Result: 初步实验显示，目标重复与更短的成功消息相关，说明可用历史可能减少通信需求；但由隐藏循环规则带来的可预测性并未缩短消息。

Conclusion: 作者假设，在历史信息能带来相同最大任务损失降低的条件下，若智能体从历史中获得更大的实际损失降低，则其所需同伴通信更少。该框架为研究记忆与通信之间的资源权衡提供了可检验的形式化工具。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [153] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiSCO是一种面向文本到图像模型的黑盒提示防御方法，通过自动扩展安全后缀并基于目标模型反馈优化提示，在不改动模型的情况下显著降低有害图像生成，同时保持图文语义一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型可能在对抗性提示下生成NSFW内容；现有白盒防御依赖模型内部访问，难以适用于闭源/专有模型；LLM提示改写等黑盒方法又难以处理“良性对抗”提示，即语言表面安全但仍因模型数据分布触发有害生成的情况。

Method: 提出DiSCO，一种零样本、严格黑盒、仅在提示层工作的即插即用防御模块。它通过目标模型自身生成的安全/不安全图像池进行对比评分，并使用分布引导的后缀扩展与束搜索优化提示；随后根据生成结果进行迭代式自适应反馈，直到获得安全内容。

Result: 在I2P基准和多种红队攻击设置下，DiSCO能稳定提升未防御模型和已有防御模型的安全性，攻击成功率分别降低37.7%和25.13%；同时保持语义一致性，并提升图像连贯性。

Conclusion: DiSCO证明了无需访问模型权重、无需训练或微调，仅通过黑盒提示级干预也能有效降低文本到图像模型的NSFW生成风险，且具备架构无关和易部署特性。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [154] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KernelArc 是一个用于自动 GPU kernel 优化的多智能体框架，通过并行策略智能体和受控信息共享提升搜索效率，在 H100/B200 上多个 SOL-ExecBench 代表任务取得榜首结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GPU kernel 优化面对异构工作负载时搜索空间巨大，单一策略或单智能体方法容易探索不足。论文旨在验证共享式多智能体搜索能否在固定候选预算内扩大探索范围并找到更强实现。

Method: 提出 KernelArc 多智能体框架：多个策略专门化智能体并行运行，通过“仅共享结论”的共享记忆、确定性 benchmark guard、只读跨智能体状态以及平台期触发的 drafting 机制进行协调；在 NVIDIA H100 与 B200 上用 SOL-ExecBench 代表性任务评测。

Result: KernelArc 生成了多类高性能实现，包括自定义 BF16 GEMM、静态 cuBLASLt Expert-API 配置表、融合 MoE backward、shape-gated decoder-layer fusion、原生 NVFP4 GQA、paged prefill attention 等；在 2026 年 7 月 30 日 SOL-ExecBench 公共榜单快照中，于代表性 L1、L2、Quantization、FlashInfer 任务排名第一。

Conclusion: 实验轨迹支持核心结论：共享多智能体搜索能够在固定候选预算下拓宽探索并达到更强 incumbent；但不同协调机制的收益依赖具体 kernel 类型和优化阶段。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [155] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CASE，用答案 token 隐藏状态训练线性选择器来替代多数投票，并提出 decodability 判断这种选择何时可信。结果显示 decodability 能有效预测选择法相对投票的收益，CASE 在中等和困难问题上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在测试时通常通过多数投票融合多个采样答案，但在难题上样本答案常存在相关错误，导致错误答案可能占多数；而基于隐藏状态选择正确候选虽有潜力，但缺乏判断其何时可靠的指标。

Method: 提出 CASE（Correctness-Axis SElection）动态选择组合器：在答案 token 的隐藏状态上训练线性门控器，并选择得分最高的候选答案。同时提出无泄漏指标 decodability，用于衡量门控器能否将同一问题的正确候选排序在错误候选之前，并采用按问题分组的评估避免 question-identity leakage。

Result: 在留出数据上，decodability 与“隐藏状态选择相对多数投票的准确率增益”高度相关（Pearson r=0.75），决策阈值约为 AUC=0.60。CASE 在通用和医学 LLM 上，在中等难度问题提升最高 19 个百分点，在困难问题提升最高 16.8 个百分点；其预测还能迁移到未见科学领域，误差在 3.8 个百分点内。

Conclusion: CASE 提供了一种比多数投票更适合部分中高难问题的测试时答案融合方法；decodability 可作为事先可测的实用标准，用于判断给定模型和任务中应采用学习式隐藏状态选择还是多数投票。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [156] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“合作式观察”框架：个人 AI 的能力取决于它能观察并压缩用户生活信息的质量，而观察权限又受系统有用性、用户信任和用户控制共同塑造。作者通过 Organizm 的六个月单用户原型案例进行初步说明，并呼吁建立评估观察质量对个人 AI 表现影响的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人 AI 若要代表用户进行规划与行动，需要理解用户目标、约束与持续承诺；但这种理解受限于系统可观察到的信息。作者指出，单纯扩大观察范围并不必然提升辅助质量，因为系统仍需对信息进行选择与压缩。

Method: 论文提出“合作式观察”（cooperative observation）框架，将个人 AI 的观察能力建模为用户与系统之间的反馈循环：系统形成用户生活的部分模型，用户评估其行为，用户的同意与控制决定系统未来可观察的范围。作者还报告了原型系统 Organizm 在六个月内的单个受试者初步使用记录，并提出后续评估方向。

Result: 初步结果以单受试者案例形式呈现，展示了个人 AI 的有用性、可检查性、信任以及未来访问权限之间可能存在相互影响：表现良好可能促使用户维持或扩展观察通道，失败则可能导致用户纠正、限制、撤销或放弃该通道。

Conclusion: 论文认为，个人智能的发展不应只关注“更多数据”，而应关注用户可控、可检查、基于信任反馈的观察机制。合作式观察可作为理解和评估个人 AI 观察质量如何影响其辅助能力的理论框架。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [157] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 KNOWSIM，通过显式建模用户知识状态及其演化来评估 LLM 的信息校准能力；实验表明其更符合人类判断，并能发现不同知识水平用户适合不同模型的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于评估和训练大语言模型的用户模拟器没有显式建模用户知识状态，难以真实反映不同知识水平用户的交互差异，也无法刻画用户知识随交互演化的过程。

Method: 论文提出 KNOWSIM 评估框架：构建一个维护显式知识状态的用户模拟器，将知识表示为由信息单元及其先修关系组成的图，并基于学习理论设计知识状态更新规则；同时从知识状态轨迹中计算知识增益、传递校准和认知过载三项指标。

Result: 在两个领域共 705 个人类-AI 会话上的验证显示，KNOWSIM 的模型排序与人类判断显著一致，符号一致率为 73–74%，优于三个基线用户模拟器；在 9 个 LLM 上的应用表明，不同用户知识水平下表现最佳的模型会发生变化。

Conclusion: KNOWSIM 能更机制化地评估 LLM 在知识密集型协作中的信息校准能力，并揭示标准评估难以发现的用户知识水平与模型能力之间的交互效应。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [158] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用 LLM 自动生成可执行的 Python 特征提取器，从 MiniZinc 模型和实例中构建图结构并提取结构特征，用于约束满足问题的算法选择；实验表明其性能优于人工 mzn2feat 和 transformer 特征方法，且特征提取过程可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束满足问题的算法选择依赖能够刻画问题结构的特征，但人工设计特征提取器需要深厚领域知识，且面对新问题类别时容易成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种自动化方法：利用大语言模型在代理式 check--fix--verify 循环中合成可执行 Python 脚本，作为可解释、面向具体问题的特征提取器。输入高层 MiniZinc 模型和实例后，LLM 生成代码构建类型化图表示，并计算图密度、变量聚类、约束紧度等结构属性。

Result: 在车辆路径、汽车排序、定长纠错码三个组合优化问题上，结合五个先进求解器组合进行评估；合成特征提取器训练出的算法选择器持续优于专家设计的 mzn2feat 特征和最佳 transformer-based trans2feat 变体，在 FLECC 上测试集准确率最高提升 8.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM 自动合成的特征提取器能够有效替代或超越人工特征工程，在保持可检查、可解释性的同时提升约束满足问题中的算法选择性能。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [159] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估26个开源小语言模型在5类安全基准上的表现，发现现有LLM安全基准用于SLMs时大量产生模糊判断，且排名对模糊样本处理极其敏感，因此不能可靠地单独证明SLMs安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLMs）越来越多部署在资源受限且隐私敏感的场景中，但现有安全/合规/偏见基准主要面向大语言模型，未必能可靠迁移到SLMs，因此论文关注这些基准是否能有效评估SLMs安全性。

Method: 作者对26个开源SLMs进行大规模评估，覆盖5个常用安全基准套件，并采用统一自动评判标准：有害回答记0分，安全回答记1分，模糊或无关回答记0.5分；同时分析模糊判定与提示复杂度、模型架构、词汇密度、困惑度、输出长度、自一致性等因素的关系，并考察排行榜对模糊样本处理方式的敏感性。

Result: 实验发现，各基准中模糊判定占主导，并与提示复杂度和模型架构相关。模糊率通常随词汇密度、输出困惑度和输出长度上升而增加，随词汇复杂度、自一致性、回答与提示相似度上升而降低。不同合理的模糊处理方式会显著改变模型排名，即使底层输出没有变化。

Conclusion: 以LLM为中心设计的安全基准不能作为SLM安全评估的独立充分证据；这些基准会混合模型能力与表面安全性，且基于平均分的排行榜在数学上不稳健，需要更适配SLMs、更能处理模糊性的评估方法。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [160] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fool’s Gold：不试图阻止开放权重模型被移除拒答机制，而是在攻击后让模型对危险请求生成看似正确但关键处错误的诱饵答案。实验显示多数大模型在保持正常能力的同时，攻击状态下显著增加错误诱饵输出，使攻击者难以判断答案是否可信；但方法不防上下文越狱，且只覆盖初始发布权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型的安全对齐可被 abliteration 等方法快速移除，现有发布时防御难以持久阻止；论文试图在无法阻止攻击的前提下，降低攻击后模型输出的可信危害性。

Method: 提出 decoy hardening（“Fool’s Gold”）：在可微分攻击仿真中训练模型，使其只在拒答机制被剥离后的攻击状态下，对危险操作请求生成流畅但关键要素被篡改的诱饵答案；同时用 refusal pin 和 benign leash 约束干净状态下的拒答、安全行为与能力保持。实验覆盖五个模型家族的七个开放权重模型（9B–122B，含稠密与 MoE）。

Result: 六个通过预注册有效性门槛的模型中，攻击状态下对保留提示的诱饵响应比例为 0.51–0.90，其中 0.27–0.84 可归因于该防御；这些模型均保持在预注册的良性行为与能力预算内。第七个较小模型未通过门槛。外部红队基准的相关切片上，防御后的 122B 在匹配质量答案中致命错误率达 0.82–0.86，而未防御模型最高约 0.10。重复采样不能可靠恢复信任：K=64 的元素级共识在部分模型上仍显著低于未防御模型，且缺乏无标签方法区分真假答案。

Conclusion: 论文主张一种“认识论”防御：既然开放权重模型的拒答机制可被移除，就让被移除后的模型输出高置信、表面可信但关键错误的危险内容，从而使攻击者无法仅凭观察表面可靠地区分正确答案与诱饵。该方法主要适用于发布时保护的权重，对上下文越狱无效，评估范围限于化学与生物危害。

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [161] [Explicit State Elicitation Is Not Enough: A Controlled Audit of Memory-Policy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17247)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Shuaiting Li,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计个性化智能体中“是否使用/忽略/更新/查询用户记忆”的结构化中间输出。结果表明，初始收益多受数据捷径、状态定义暴露和标签条件化影响；显式状态输出字段没有显著提升策略准确率，也不能证明模型具备忠实内部机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体在执行当前任务前，需要判断检索到的用户记忆应被使用、忽略、更新还是进一步查询；论文借此审计结构化中间输出是否真正提升决策，而非依赖数据捷径或标签条件化。

Method: 作者提出经验审计协议：检查数据集捷径、隔离提示词 bundle 中的变化、检验中间状态标签是否与答案强关联、测试分解语义证据、并审计提供商级执行失败。先用480例合成开发集诊断，再构造冻结的160例受控反事实集，包含40组四路匹配 family 和规则派生参考策略。

Result: 开发集上状态结构化提示看似带来较大收益，但TF-IDF显示存在词汇可分性且缺少正向 standalone Ignore 案例。受控集上，暴露四个状态定义提高准确率，但单独加入显式状态输出字段对 Llama-3.3-70B 无显著提升，对 GPT-OSS-120B 仅有边际且不显著提升。提供 benchmark 关联状态标签会改变策略预测，但因标签确定性映射到策略，这只能说明标签条件化，不能证明忠实内部机制。family级与seed稳定性分析显示，样本级准确率高估了反事实一致性；完整四路family成功很少。分解语义证据未改善干净评估端点的路由，GPT-OSS对应条件因提供商请求校验不可用。

Conclusion: 论文认为，结构化中间输出的表面收益需要通过反事实控制、标签关联性和执行失败审计来严格验证；显式中间状态字段本身未被证明能稳定提升策略分类或反映忠实推理机制。研究范围仅限策略分类，不评估下游响应、工具调用或记忆库变更。

Abstract: Personalized agents must decide whether retrieved user memory should be used, ignored, updated, or queried before it affects a current task. We use this setting to develop an empirical audit protocol for structured intermediate outputs: first audit dataset shortcuts, then isolate bundled prompt changes, check whether intermediate labels are answer-associated, test decomposed semantic evidence, and audit provider-level execution failures. A 480-example synthetic development set initially suggested large gains from a state-structured prompt bundle, but TF-IDF diagnostics showed lexical separability and no positive standalone Ignore cases. We therefore construct a frozen 160-example controlled counterfactual set with 40 matched four-way families and rule-derived reference policies. On this set, exposing the four state definitions improves accuracy, but an isolated explicit state-output field does not significantly improve policy accuracy for Llama-3.3-70B and gives only a marginal, non-significant gain for GPT-OSS-120B. Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [162] [Do LLMs Know a Good Hypothesis When They See One? Logit-Based Energy Scoring Outperforms Prompted LLM-as-Judge for Scientific Hypothesis Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17270)
*Swati Rajwal,Sanjay Das,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用语言模型 logit 能量分数来评估科学假设，避免依赖 LLM 评审或语义相似度。实验显示该方法整体优于提示式排序，但最佳结果来自事后挑选的配置，因此仍需进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于科学假设生成，但如何可信地评估生成假设仍是难题。现有方法常依赖 LLM 评审或语义相似度，可能偏向熟悉而非新颖的想法。

Method: 论文提出一种基于 logit 的能量评分方法，使用语言模型的内在置信度评估假设，而不是进行提示式比较判断。作者在 12 个学科的 1,323 篇论文上测试 7 个语言模型；每篇论文配对真实假设和 15 个错误替代项，并比较内在评分与提示式 listwise ranking。

Result: 内在评分在两个评分器汇总下达到 33.0% Hit@1，高于提示式列表排序的 16.6%。最强配置为 10 亿参数模型结合 logit-based energy scoring，达到 53.1%，但这是在 14 个模型-评分器组合中事后选择的最高结果。

Conclusion: 基于模型内在置信度的评分方法在科学假设评估中具有潜力，可能比传统提示式判断更有效，但最优结果存在事后选择因素，需要进一步验证其稳健性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for scientific hypothesis generation. However, evaluating generated hypotheses remains a challenge for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific workflows. Existing approaches often use LLMs as judges or rely on semantic similarity, which can favor familiar ideas over novel ones. We propose a logit-based energy scoring method that evaluates hypotheses using a language model's intrinsic confidence rather than comparative judgment. We benchmarked seven language models on 1,323 papers across 12 disciplines. Each paper was paired with its hypothesis and fifteen incorrect alternatives. Intrinsic scoring reached 33.0% Hit@1 pooled across both scorers, compared with 16.6% for prompted listwise ranking. The strongest configuration, a 1-billion-parameter model using logit-based energy scoring, reached 53.1%, though this was the maximum across 14 model-by-scorer combinations selected post hoc. Overall, intrinsic model confidence shows potential for scientific hypothesis evaluation. This study also motivates future research on confidence-based methods for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [163] [ASI-Bench: At the Dawn of Artificial Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17271)
*Junwei Zhou,Zhen Sun,Binyu Li,Jiangyu Zhou,Yuexi Pan,Hengyu Wang,Honghe Ren,Xiaohan Jia,Xueyang Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Yongchao Chen,Yuanning Feng,Junhao Wu,Cheng Zhang,Sijia Chen,Haoyu Xue,Chengsong You,Huan Wang,Koutian Wu,Peigan Gao,Jiakun Wu,Wenzhe Li,Ergan Shang,Qingyuan Zheng,Jingjing Zhou,Ruixuan Jia,Yan Xu,Hongrui Zhang,Xiao-Han Ma,Zhengxiang Cheng,Yuexing Hao,Liting Mai,Xianglin Ji,Wenjun Zhang,Zhuofan Chen,Yixiao Huang,Chi Wang,Wenyue Hua,Yilun Hao,Yuantao Zhai,Ziyan Zhao,Jingyan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASI-Bench 是一个评估 AI 自主科研能力的新基准，覆盖多领域项目级任务，并通过逐步撤除人类方法指导来测试模型独立科研能力；结果表明当前先进 AI 在缺少指导时表现显著下降，仍远未达到自主科学研究水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 主要依赖学习、压缩和应用既有人类知识，而现有评测多关注知识问答或在人类强指导下完成任务，难以衡量 AI 是否具备面向未知问题的创新探索与自主科研执行能力。

Method: 论文提出 ASI-Bench：一个项目级科研任务基准，覆盖 11 个科学领域的 60 个研究任务。其核心设计是在同一研究项目中逐步减少人类方法学指导，以测试 AI 能否自主选择方法、开展研究并产出可验证结果。任务构建经过专家评审、AI 辅助审计、沙盒执行和评分器验证。

Result: 在 18 种先进 agent--model 配置上，平均分从完整方法学指导下的 50.91 降至仅指定方法时的 29.10，并在需要模型自行决定方法时进一步降至 26.62，显示当前系统对人类指导高度依赖。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 系统距离自主完成端到端、项目级科学研究仍有明显差距。ASI-Bench 为评估和推动 AI 的创新探索与自主科研能力提供了开放基准。

Abstract: Artificial superintelligence (ASI) requires AI to move beyond mastering existing knowledge toward exploring the unknown, creating new knowledge, and turning new ideas into verifiable results. However, the capabilities of today's AI systems are still largely built on learning, compressing, and applying existing human knowledge. Accordingly, existing benchmarks primarily test whether AI can produce correct answers based on learned knowledge, or whether it can complete tasks under extensive human guidance. We therefore introduce ASI-Bench, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate AI systems' capabilities of innovative exploration and autonomous scientific execution across general research domains, and the first to progressively withdraw human methodological guidance within the same research project to test how far AI can proceed on its own. Built by over 40 experts with the cost of 31,000+ human hours, ASI-Bench contains 60 project-level research tasks across 11 scientific domains and progressively reduces methodological guidance to test whether AI can independently select methods, conduct research, and produce verifiable results. All tasks undergo expert review, AI-assisted auditing, sandbox execution, and scorer validation. Across 18 state-of-the-art agent--model configurations, the average score drops from 50.91 with full methodological guidance to 29.10 with only the method specified and 26.62 when agents must determine the method themselves. This sharp decline shows that current systems remain heavily dependent on human guidance and are still far from autonomously conducting end-to-end, project-level scientific research. ASI-Bench is open to the world. We invite researchers and builders everywhere to contribute new tasks, challenge the limits of today's AI, and help accelerate humanity's collective path toward artificial superintelligence at https://asibench.apexin.ai/submit.

</details>


### [164] [DeAR: Decentralized Agentic Reasoning via Capability Grounding and Collaborative Thought Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17282)
*Xing Wei,Changmeng Zheng,XiaoYong Wei,Xiufen Ye,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeAR 是一种去中心化多智能体推理框架，通过动态能力匹配、思维图导航和拓扑更新实现自适应协作，在多模态与文本 QA 基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推理系统多依赖中心化协议，容易产生路由瓶颈与固定角色分配问题，在复杂多模态查询中适应性不足、鲁棒性较弱。

Method: 提出 DeAR（Decentralized Agentic Reasoning）框架，将中心控制转向自主的点对点协作。核心机制包括：去中心化能力 grounding 以实现查询相关的智能体专长分配；thought map navigation 用于有目标的同伴交互；topology update 用于自适应错误修正。

Result: 在 9 个多样化的多模态推理与文本问答基准上，DeAR 相比近期基线方法持续取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 实验表明，去中心化且自适应的多智能体协作能够提升知识密集型推理任务的准确率，并缓解中心化方案中的瓶颈与静态分工问题。

Abstract: Existing agentic reasoning systems typically rely on centralized protocols. This design introduces routing bottlenecks and static role allocations that often fail when handling complex multimodal queries. We propose DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning), a framework that shifts from central control to autonomous peer-to-peer collaboration. DeAR is built on three mechanisms: (1) decentralized capability grounding for query-dependent agent specialization, (2) thought map navigation for targeted peer interactions, and (3) topology update for adaptive error correction. Evaluations across 9 diverse multimodal reasoning and text-based QA benchmarks indicate that DeAR consistently outperforms recent baseline methods, validating that decentralized and adaptive collaboration among agents enhances accuracy in knowledge-intensive reasoning tasks. The source code will be available at https://open_upon_acceptance.

</details>


### [165] [PlanPO: Group Planning-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17289)
*Dayang Liang,Liyuan He,Xuan Feng,Shuxin Li,Bo An,Yunlong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanPO 通过在成功轨迹中比较整体交互长度和单轮响应长度，为多轮任务中的 LLM 强化学习提供更细粒度优势信号，显著提升规划效率和任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 group-relative policy optimization 方法在多轮交互任务中，通常只按最终成功与否分配奖励，无法区分同样成功但效率差异很大的轨迹，导致成功轨迹优势信号塌缩，限制性能提升。

Method: 提出 Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization（PlanPO）。该方法在 group-relative 优化框架中引入 coarse-to-fine advantage signals：在同一任务的成功采样轨迹内，比较轨迹级长度差异与轮次级响应长度差异，从而为成功轨迹提供更细粒度的相对优势信号。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 SciWorld 三个多轮交互基准上，PlanPO 相比 GRPO 平均提升 27.2%，优于近期强基线方法，同时几乎不增加额外训练成本。

Conclusion: PlanPO 能有效缓解成功轨迹优势塌缩问题，使智能体从高质量 rollout 中学习更具泛化性的交互规划与文本生成行为，而不是简单退化为长度最小化。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization has emerged as a key paradigm for training agentic large language models (LLMs) on multi-turn interactive tasks. However, most existing variants fail to distinguish advantages among successful trajectories even when these trajectories differ substantially in their interaction efficiency. For instance, circuitous successes are often assigned the identical outcome reward, causing advantage collapse and severe performance bottlenecks. To this end, we propose Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization (PlanPO), a simple yet effective RL method for learning generalizable planning abilities beyond task-specific high-quality behavior patterns. Specifically, PlanPO introduces coarse-to-fine advantage signals, which capture the relative differences in trajectory-level lengths and turn-level response lengths conditioned on successful trajectories sampled for the same task. Within the group-relative optimization structure, this enables agents to actively learn generalizable and deliberate behaviors spanning interaction planning and textual generation from high-quality rollouts, without degenerating into vanilla length minimization. Experimentally, PlanPO improves over GRPO by 27.2\% on average across the challenging multi-turn benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, outperforming recent powerful baselines while incurring negligible additional training cost.

</details>


### [166] [LiveHouse-TS: An Open-world Living Benchmark for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17299)
*Haomin Wen,Ziyu Zhou,Qingxiang Liu,Siru Zhong,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiveHouse-TS，一个用于时间序列基础模型的开放世界动态基准，通过真实未来数据流进行连续评测，发现静态基准下的模型排名在真实动态环境中会显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）评测主要依赖固定历史测试窗口的静态基准，只能给出某一固定历史条件下的平均性能快照，难以反映真实开放环境中持续演化的数据特征，如季节变化、分布漂移和突发事件。因此，论文旨在建立一种能持续检验模型时间有效性和鲁棒性的动态评测框架。

Method: 论文提出 LiveHouse-TS，这是面向 TSFMs 的首个开放世界动态基准基础设施。其核心方法是在真实未来数据流上采用 prequential（预测后验证）评估协议，对模型进行连续、在线式评测，而不是一次性静态排行榜评估。该框架覆盖 11 个领域、17 个数据集，用于长期观察模型排序、鲁棒性和分布漂移下的表现。

Result: 大规模流式评测表明，在 LiveHouse-TS 的动态评测协议下，基于静态基准得到的模型排名会发生显著重排，说明静态排行榜不能可靠代表模型在真实持续环境中的长期表现。

Conclusion: 论文认为，TSFM 评测应从静态快照式准确率转向连续时间有效性评估。LiveHouse-TS 为研究模型长期排名稳定性、分布漂移鲁棒性和开放世界泛化能力提供了新的基础设施。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as a highly promising paradigm for cross-domain zero-shot forecasting. However, existing evaluation protocols predominantly rely on static benchmarks with fixed historical test windows. While these benchmarks provide a valuable baseline snapshot, they evaluate an average performance on a fixed history, failing to capture how models behave in continuously evolving real-world environments characterized by seasonal variations, distribution shifts, and unexpected events. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiveHouse-TS, the first open-world living benchmark infrastructure for TSFMs. By evaluating models prequentially on real future data in open-world environments, LiveHouse-TS shifts time series benchmarking from snapshot accuracy to continuous temporal validity. Rather than acting as a one-off leaderboard, our infrastructure serves as a continuous time series infrastructure designed to explore vital, long-term scientific questions: Can model rankings be maintained over the long term? Which models remain genuinely robust under distribution shifts? Extensive streaming evaluations across 11 domains with 17 datasets demonstrate that static rankings undergo a dramatic reshuffling under a live protocol.

</details>


### [167] [SignalReasoner: Assessing the Upper Bound of 3B Models for Signal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17301)
*Guozheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用 SFT 和基于可验证奖励的 RL 提升 Qwen2.5-3B-Base 在无线信号数学题上的推理能力，并比较 GRPO、GSPO、GMPO。最佳模型准确率从 12.37% 提升到 39.12%，显示领域思维链初始化与强化微调对信号数学推理有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经过监督式思维链微调和基于可验证奖励的强化学习后，在数学推理上显著提升；但这些方法在信号处理/无线通信数学问题中的适用性仍研究不足。论文旨在探索如何将 Qwen2.5-3B-Base 适配到 WirelessMATHBench-XL 中的研究生级信号数学推理任务。

Method: 作者比较两种训练范式：一是直接在 WirelessMATHBench-XL 上使用可验证奖励进行强化学习；二是先用无线领域蒸馏得到的思维链语料进行监督微调，再执行同样的领域强化学习。强化学习算法方面，对比 GRPO、GSPO 和 GMPO，以评估不同策略优化方法在信号推理任务中的稳定性与准确率。

Result: 最佳模型整体准确率达到 39.12%，相比未训练的 Base 模型 12.37% 提升超过三倍，说明领域适配训练对无线/信号数学推理能力有显著帮助。摘要未给出各算法和两种训练范式的详细分项结果。
